# Synonyms
> Total Questions: 2012

---

[Q1] HIND
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Fore
(b) Anterior
(c) Ventral
(d) Rear
[ANS] d
[SOL]
📘 **English**: 'Rear' is the closest synonym for 'HIND'.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'HIND' का अर्थ (पिछला / पीछे का भाग (Rear / back part)) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Rear'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Fore • (B) Anterior • (C) Ventral • (D) Rear

[Q5] PREDILECTION
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 11/12/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) special liking
(b) dreamy meditation
(c) observant nature
(d) extreme indifference
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Predilection- special liking. Dilemma- a difficult situation where a choice must be made between two or more options. Quandary- a state of confusion or uncertainty about what to do. Harmony- a peaceful or balanced state of agreement. Solution- an answer or way of resolving a problem. Certainty- a state of being sure or confident about something. Tribulations- severe difficulties or troubles.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'PREDILECTION' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'special liking'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) special liking • (B) dreamy meditation • (C) observant nature • (D) extreme indifference

[Q8] TEETOTALLER
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 11/12/2024 (Afternoon)]

[ANS] b

[Q22] HARASS
[EXAM: SSC CGL 09/09/2024 (1 s t Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) betray
(b) relish
(c) soothe
(d) intimidate
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Intimidate- to frighten or make someone feel afraid. Harass- to disturb or bother someone repeatedly. Betray- to reveal or disclose information that is meant to be kept secret. Relish- to enjoy or take great pleasure in something. Soothe- to calm or alleviate pain or discomfort.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'HARASS' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'intimidate' (डराना / धमकाना / भयभीत करना) इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) betray: धोखा देना • (B) relish • (C) soothe: शांत करना • (D) intimidate: डराना / धमकाना / भयभीत करना

[Q24] ALTRUISTIC
[EXAM: SSC CGL 09/09/2024 (1 s t Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) philanthropic
(b) phantasm al
(c) phenomenal
(d) ph ono typical
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Philanthropic- seeking to promote the welfare of others, especially through the donation of money to good causes. Altruistic- showing selfless concern for the well-being of others. Phantasm al- not existing in nature or subject to explanation according to natural laws. Phenomenal- remarkable or exceptional, especially exceptionally good. Ph ono typical- relating to or denoting a system of writing in which each symbol represents a spoken sound.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'ALTRUISTIC' का अर्थ (परोपकारी) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'philanthropic'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) philanthropic • (B) phantasm al • (C) phenomenal • (D) ph ono typical

[Q195] PRUDENT
[EXAM: SSC CPO 29/06/2024 (3 r d Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Diffident
(b) Precious
(c) Doubtful
(d) Judicious Solutions
[ANS] c
[SOL]
honesty (d) Count- to calculate or consider something numerically. (c) Inhumanly- lacking compassion or showing cruelty. (b) Smart- having or showing quick-witted intelligence. (a) Noise- a loud or unpleasant sound. (a) Resistant- able to withstand or fight against something. (a) Committed- dedicated or devoted to a cause or activity. (a) Denounce- to publicly declare something as wrong or evil. (b) Fading- gradually grow faint and disappear. (b) Censure- to express severe disapproval of something. (b) Dread- to be very afraid of or worried about something. (d) Greedy- having an intense desire for something, especially food. (a) Elegant- graceful and stylish in appearance or manner. (a) 
• Soothe - to comfort somebody. (d) 
• Occult - relating to mystical, magical, or supernatural phenomena. (c) Dirty- covered with or characterized by dirt or impurities. Synonyms Frightening- causing fear or alarm. Censure- to strongly disapprove or criticize something. Persuade- to convince someone to do something. Compel- to force someone to do something. Foster- to encourage or promote the development of something. Dread- to be very afraid of or worried about something. Mourn- to feel and show great sadness. Approve- to be pleased about something. Amuse- to feel great sadness. Greedy- having an intense desire for something, especially food. Voracious- very eager for something, especially a lot of food. Compensated- satisfied thirst or desire. Appreciate- to recognize the value or significance of something. Elegant- graceful and stylish in appearance or manner. Sable- of high quality or very thin or delicate. Vulgar- lacking sophistication or good taste. 
• Soothe - to comfort somebody. Pacify- to make somebody who is angry or upset be calm or quiet. Strengthen- to make something or someone stronger. Blame- responsibility for something bad. Rebuke- to speak angrily to somebody. 
• Occult - relating to mystical, magical, or supernatural phenomena. Supernatural- attributed to some force beyond scientific understanding or the laws of nature. Questionable- doubtful or open to question. Evident- clearly seen or understood. Obtuse- covered with or characterized by dirt or impurities. Filthy- extremely dirty or unpleasant. Tidy- neat and well-organized. Shiny- Visual- relating to seeing or sight. (d) Judicious- having or showing reason and good judgment in making decisions. Prudent- sensible and careful when making judgements and decisions. Diffident- modest or shy because of a lack of self-confidence. Precious- very costly or important. Doubtful- in a doubt. SSC Selection Post (Phase - XII)
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'PRUDENT' का अर्थ (विवेकी / समझदार (Wise and careful)) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Doubtful'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

[Q217] ANCHORITES
[EXAM: SSC P Y Q]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) wanderers
(b) pirates
(c) hermits
(d) sailors
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Hermits - individuals who choose to live in seclusion, often for religious or spiritual reasons. 
• Anchorites - religious recluse who lives alone, often in seclusion for spiritual reasons. 
• Wanderers - individuals who travel without a fixed destination or purpose. 
• Pirates - people who engage in acts of robbery and violence at sea. 
• Sailors - people who navigate or work on ships at sea.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'ANCHORITES' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'hermits'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) wanderers • (B) pirates • (C) hermits • (D) sailors

[Q499] VOGUE
[EXAM: SSC CGL Tier-2 02/03/2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) demand
(b) obscurity
(c) standard
(d) fashion
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Vogue - the popular style or fashion at a specified time 
• Fashion - the style of dressing or behaving that is the most popular at a particular time 
• Demand - a strong request or order that must be obeyed 
• Obscurity - the state of not being known to many people 
• Standard - something that people generally accept as normal and correct (d)
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'VOGUE' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'fashion'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

[SOL]
Serendipity - the fact of finding something valuable or interesting by accident or something pleasant happening by chance 
• Godsend - something unexpected that is very useful because it comes just when it is needed 
• Sibilance - a hissing quality of sound, or the hissing sound itself 
• Catastrophe - a sudden disaster that causes great suffering or damage 
• Surreptitious - done secretly (b)
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'SERENDIPITY' का अर्थ (नसीब) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Godsend'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Sibilance • (B) Godsend • (C) Catastrophe: तबाही • (D) Surreptitious: छल से किया हुआ

[Q699] GENERIC
[EXAM: SSC CGL 13/8/2021 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Specific
(b) Precise
(c) Definite
(d) Universal
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Generic - shared by, including, or typical of a whole group of things 
• Specific - detailed or exact 
• Precise - clear and accurate 
• Definite - fixed and unlikely to change, certain 
• Universal - connected with, done by, or affecting everyone in the world or everyone in a particular group
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'GENERIC' का अर्थ (सामान्य) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Universal'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

(a) neglect
(b) estimation
(c) accuracy
(d) vagueness
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Accuracy - the fact of being correct without any mistake 
• Precision - the quality of being clear or exact 
• Neglect - to give too little or no attention or care to somebody/something 
• Estimation - opinion or judgment 
• Vagueness - the quality of not being clearly expressed
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'PRECISION' का अर्थ (शुद्धता) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'accuracy'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

[Q736] VACILLATE
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 10/8/2021 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Augment
(b) Waver
(c) Strengthen
(d) Fortify
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Vacillate - waver between different opinions or actions; be indecisive. 
• Augment - make (something) greater by adding to it; increase. 
• Strengthen - make or become stronger. 
• Fortify - strengthen (someone) mentally or physically. Here strengthen and fortify are synonyms and waver is the synonym of vacillate. (b)
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'VACILLATE' का अर्थ (संदेह करना) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Waver'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

[Q751] FLABBERGASTED
[EXAM: SSC CPO 25/11/2020 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) motivated
(b) disturbed
(c) Terrified
(d) Astonished
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Disturbed- to want to have something very much (especially something that belongs (b) to somebody else). Renounced- to say formally that you no longer want to have something or an act of sacrificing. Despised- Poke- Shove- Halt- Shout- Uproar- a lot of noise, confusion, anger or an angry discussion about something. Tumult- a loud, confused noise, especially one caused by a large mass of people. Peace- Clarity- Chaste- Divine- Pure- something clean and not containing any harmful substances, not mixed with anything else. Defiled- Liberated- Flabbergasted- Astonished- Terrified- Fantasy- Fiction- stories, novels, etc. which describe events and people that are not real. Truth- Literature- writing that is considered to be a work of art. Literature includes novels, plays and poetry. Forgive- a conscious, deliberate decision to release feelings of resentment or vengeance toward a person or group who has harmed you. Condone- to accept or agree with something that most people think is wrong. Forbid- to not allow something, to order somebody not to do something Impose- to make a law, rule, opinion, etc. be accepted by using your power or authority Opulent- Sumptuous- Bereft- Stingy- someone not generous, unwilling to give, especially money. Meager- Wrest- Deter- to make somebody decide not to do something, especially by telling him/her that it would have bad results. Flashy- attracting attention by being very big, bright and expensive. Gaudy- Plain- Towering- extremely tall, especially in comparison with the surroundings. Lofty- Humble- not thinking that you are better or more important than other people, not proud. Plain- Silent- Desired- Coveted- to want to have something very much (especially something that belongs to somebody else). to say formally that you no longer want to have something or an act of sacrificing. Despised- to push somebody/something with a finger, stick. Shove- a short stop in some activity, movement or growth. Shout- a lot of noise, confusion, anger or an angry discussion about something. Tumult- a loud, confused noise, especially one caused by a large mass of people. Peace- the state of being calm or quiet. Clarity- the quality of being clear and easy to understand. Chaste- pure, formal Divine- something clean and not containing any harmful substances, not mixed with anything else. Defiled- free from traditional opinions Flabbergasted- very surprised/shocking. Terrified- very afraid
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'FLABBERGASTED' का अर्थ (हैरत में डाला हुआ) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'disturbed'  इसका सबसे उपयुक्त समानार्थक (Synonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) motivated • (B) disturbed • (C) Terrified • (D) Astonished

Zenith means the time at which something is most powerful or successful (the peak). 'Summit' represents the highest point.

[RULE] Positive/Negative Connotation Match

(c) Realistic
(d) Visionary
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Pragmatic means dealing with things sensibly and realistically rather than on theoretical considerations. 'Realistic' is the correct match.
उदा. Pragmatic का अर्थ व्यावहारिक और यथार्थवादी होता है। 'Realistic' इसका सही पर्याय है।
